- **ethereum**: - Chain `eip155:1`. Proof-of-stake: 12 s slots; blocks are final after two epochs (about 13 minutes). FloFi treats a receipt as "confirmed onchain", not as reconciled completion. - Mainnet gas is the costliest of FloFi's networks: Review shows the fee budget (`gasLimit × maxFeePerGas`) and the owner's ETH must cover it plus any native amount. - Native ETH and WETH are different assets. A swap side ([FloFi-xyz/FloFi](https://github.com/FloFi-xyz/FloFi/tree/HEAD/skills/networks/ethereum) · ★4)
